It is therefore important to mention the ongoing dispute over the rule of law, which is reflected in the lack of decision to mobilise funds for Poland under the National Reconstruction Plan. The European Commission does not want to release funds from the Reconstruction Fund until the Polish government withdraws from the changes in the judicial system considered to restrict the independence of courts in Poland. Let us remind you that in the case of Poland it is about the amount of EUR 58 billion, which at the current euro-to-gold rate gives the amount of more than PLN 270 billion. These funds could already be used by entrepreneurs, local governments, farmers. Thanks to our presence in EU structures, but also in NATO, we have a constant sense of stability, security and many economic and social benefits that result from our membership in the Community. Therefore, our presence in the Union and NATO guarantees us full protection in terms of economic, external and defence security. I think I will end with this, although it would still be worth mentioning a number of important events (the Bell), what gives a common European market, the free movement of goods, services, capital, people, the great opportunity for young people, Erasmus, the exchange and finally the security that I have been talking about, which is connected with our presence in the Union, the presence in NATO. We are probably asking ourselves what situation Poland would be in if it were not part of these two important international organisations. Because such an organization is the European Union, which we are creating together and which form we influence, but also this important defence structure, which is the North Atlantic Pact, NATO. Thank you so much for your attention. [[Applause]] [[Bravo!]]

ParlaMint-PL_2022-04-28-sejm-53-2.u412	Dear Marshal! The Polish Army has been stationed in Ciechanów since 1918. In the imperial barracks, 32 were stationed. Ciechanowski Infantry Regiment, and in the years 1921-1939 the pride of our city - 11. The regiment of the Legion Halls. Marshal Edward Chopper-Rydz. We did it. 5. Mazovian Territorial Defence Brigade. d.o.b. Mieczysława Dziemiewicza ps. The swarm is with us, and the historic barracks have been saved. Helping citizens during the pandemic, effectively protecting the Polish-Belarusian border from thousands of immigrants trying to cross our borders illegally, the borders of the European Union are proof of how effective and necessary the Territorial Defence Forces are. It is also worth mentioning their role in the context of the war in Ukraine. The help they bring to those fleeing the brutal aggression of Putin Ukrainians is invaluable. ParlaMint-PL_2022-04-28-sejm-53-2.u413	Declaration on the World Day for Safety and Health at Work In my statement, I would like to draw attention to the World Day of Safety and Health at Work celebrated on 28 April. This day is celebrated on an international, national, regional and local level - each year with a special motto. Special attention is then paid to preventing accidents and protecting workers from occupational diseases by identifying existing and new risks, creating prevention programmes, disseminating the latest knowledge in the field of health and safety and promoting compliance with health and safety rules at all levels in the work process. In parallel, the International Day of Remembrance for Victims of Accidents at Work and Occupational Diseases is held to commemorate all those who have suffered during their work. Every year in Poland, about 300 people are killed by accidents at work, and 600 are seriously injured, often irreversible. The main causes of events and accidents are: bad and inappropriate organisation of work and workplaces, lack of training in health and safety, limited focus on the work done. Wide dissemination of knowledge about health and safety, not only before the start of work, but also during it, is very necessary, all the more so as the development of industry and services brings new occupational risks.
ParlaMint-PL_2022-04-28-sejm-53-2.u414	In 2023 it will be 100 years since the birth of Jerzy Nowosielski. Jerzy Nowosielski, born in 1923 in Krakow, was one of the most outstanding figures of Polish contemporary culture. Painting, theoretical thought, theology, artistic pedagogy, activities to promote and support art - these are just some of the areas in which Jerzy Nowosielski showed unfailing activity and had undisputed successes. In 1945-1947 he studied at the Faculty of Painting at the Academy of Fine Arts in Krakow. In 1945-1949 he was a member of the Group of Young Plastics and since 1957 one of the founding members of the Artistic Association Krakowska Group. He was associated with the Krakow Academy of Fine Arts, where he worked as a professor in 1967-1993. Jerzy Nowosielski received the honorary doctorate of the Jagiellonian University as the only artist (2000) after Jan Matejce and the Academy of Fine Arts in Lviv (2008) and numerous awards, medals, national awards, among others. The Great Cross of the Order of Rebirth of Poland (1998), the Medal of Cracovia Merenti (1999), the Golden Medal of Deserving Culture Gloria Artis ¿ (2008). The Polish Orthodox Church honored it with a medal of St. Maria Magdalena II (1985) and the Cross of Konstanty Ostrogski (1985). He received among others. Their reward. Włodzimierz Pietrzak (Publishing Institute of PAK, Warsaw 1967), Minister of Culture and Art Award for Art Art in the Field of Painting (1973), Polish Academy of Sciences Award. Albert's Brother (1977), The Award Alfreda Jurzykowski (USA, 1981), Minister of Culture and First Art Award (1981), State Prize of the First Degree (1984), Artistic Award of the Warsaw Archdiocese Museum (1985), City Award of Krakow (1987), Grand Culture Foundation Award in Warsaw (1994), Award of the Polish Academy of Sciences. Jan Cybis (1998), The Award Witold Wojtkiewicz awarded by the Management Board of the District of ZPAP in Krakow (1999), the Totus Tuus Award in the category of achievements in the field of Christian culture (2000), the Prize for Christian Culture (2000). Erasmus and Anna Jerzmanowski, so-called. Polish Nobel, awarded by the Polish Academy of Skills (2010). Jerzy Nowosielski's artistic work was highly rated by critics on more than 100 individual exhibitions and more than 250 collective exhibitions. Among the most important Polish individual exhibitions are exhibitions at the National Museum in Poznań and BWA in Kraków (1993), the National Museum in Wrocław (1994), Zachęcie in Warsaw (2003), as well as the State Art Gallery in Sopot (2021-2022). The artist represented Poland at the Venice Biennale (1956) and Säo Paulo (1959). Jerzy Nowosielski was the author of many texts about art and faith. He is the author of equipment and polychromes of numerous sacral objects for churches: Roman Catholic, Greek Catholic and Orthodox. In his secular work he derived from the achievements of the avant-garde art and the tradition of Byzantine painting. He often referred to the subject of geometric abstraction, feminine act and landscape. Jerzy Nowosielski died in Kraków in 2011. His art is still considered a unique phenomenon, combining the sacrum and profanum zones. Among the most outstanding works of the artist of secular character are among others. The Battle of Addis Abeba (1947), the Swimmers (1953), the Cellist (1959), the Mystery of the Fiancée (1962), the Villa dei Misteri (1975), the Girls on the Ship (1981), the Tibetan Beach (1983).
